## 2026-11-27 — Research erasure inbox receipt race remediation
- **Finding:** `executeResearchSubjectErasure` previously used random receipt IDs and `INSERT OR REPLACE` against a table with UNIQUE(`outbox_id`). Two concurrent deliveries could both pass the initial lookup, then replace each other's inbox row after returning different receipt IDs. This could make a USER_DB outbox receipt refer to an inbox receipt that was subsequently replaced.
- **Change:** Stable `erasure_id`/`receipt_id` derived from the durable `outbox_id`; `INSERT OR IGNORE` preserves the first committed inbox row; a post-transaction read validates and returns the canonical receipt. Added regression assertions that duplicate delivery retains exactly one canonical receipt.
- **Files:** `worker/research/researchService.ts`, `tests/rt-db-triad-001.test.ts`.
- **Status:** IMPLEMENTED_BUT_NOT_VERIFIED. No local test/typecheck execution because the connected MacBook is Offline. The added regression currently covers duplicate delivery/replay; a deterministic overlapping-delivery test remains required.
- **Code commits:** `8c014f249d61ead95ec005974a29d900bb907e60`, `a3713a1f05eb03639c127c6d995f805839f9292e`.


## 2026-11-27 — Research erasure inbox identity-conflict hardening
- **Finding:** The completed-inbox fast path in executeResearchSubjectErasure returned a stored receipt using only outbox_id; it did not verify that the row belonged to the supplied subject_key and subject_key_version. Reusing/corrupting an outbox ID could therefore return a receipt for a different pseudonymous subject.
- **Change:** The initial inbox lookup now reads subject identity and rejects mismatches with RESEARCH_SUBJECT_ERASURE_OUTBOX_IDENTITY_CONFLICT before returning any completed receipt. Added a regression assertion for a completed outbox ID replayed against a different subject key.
- **Files:** worker/research/researchService.ts, tests/rt-db-triad-001.test.ts.
- **Verification:** Source and migration contract reviewed. Test/typecheck not executed because local execution device remains Offline. Status remains IMPLEMENTED_BUT_NOT_VERIFIED.
- **GitHub:** PR #78 remains Draft. Latest code commit: 0f1818e488e12580bfc9c01356cd9de04d3970fc; execution-state reconciliation commit: 399a93a2de17ccddf914a7981d46981567001fe3.
- **CI:** PR workflow run 45980515216 failed at detect; downstream api/frontend/contracts gates were skipped. A Cloudflare Workers Builds check for radartehran-frontend also failed. Job logs still return BlobNotFound, so failure causes are not attributed to source changes.

## 2026-11-27 — Concurrent cross-subject erasure collision fix
- **Finding:** A deeper race existed beyond duplicate receipt replacement. Two Workers could both read an empty inbox for the same outbox_id but different subject keys. The old D1 batch deleted the requested subject's events before INSERT OR IGNORE established which subject owned that outbox ID; the losing request could therefore delete another subject's events and only then fail canonical receipt validation.
- **Fix:** Reordered the atomic D1 batch to claim/insert the inbox identity first. Research event deletion and aggregation invalidation now require an EXISTS row matching the exact outbox_id + subject_key + subject_key_version. Reordered replay detection to inspect batch result index 0 (the inbox insert).
- **Regression:** Added a deterministic two-delivery test. A barrier forces both callers to observe an empty inbox; D1 batches are serialized; the test requires one winner, one rejected identity collision, zero remaining events for the winner, and the losing subject's event preserved.
- **Files:** worker/research/researchService.ts; tests/rt-db-triad-001.test.ts.
- **Verification:** Source, D1 schema and generated outbox ID format reviewed. Tests/typecheck not run because the connected MacBook remains Offline. Status: IMPLEMENTED_BUT_NOT_VERIFIED.
- **GitHub:** PR #78 remains Draft. Code head: 2b71c9a1eac38f02255f58c8676926de49a490de; execution-state update commit: 41b29102f964159a4ecc6d7c6fdebbabd109224a.
- **CI:** On code head 2b71c9a, runs 45986000419 (Tehran Radar CI Quality Gates), 45986000574 (Cloudflare Native Auth Validation), 45986000519 (Path-Filtered Checks), and 45986000654 (Radar Backend Triad) failed. E2E/downstream path jobs were skipped. Job-step arrays are empty and log downloads return BlobNotFound; root causes remain unknown.
- **Next:** Execute focused test and Worker typecheck; diagnose CI; then inspect USER_DB outbox concurrent status regressions and continue Express Firestore adapter retirement.


## 2026-11-27 — USER_DB outbox stale-worker state protection
- **Finding:** processPendingResearchErasureOutbox updated the outbox row to RETRY_PENDING in its catch path without checking the current status. A stale Worker that failed after a competing Worker committed COMPLETED could downgrade the durable deletion state.
- **Fix:** Retry transition now applies only while the outbox status is PENDING or RETRY_PENDING. If no row is updated, the worker reads the current state; a valid COMPLETED row is counted as completed using its durable erased_events_count instead of being downgraded.
- **Regression:** Added a deterministic stale-read/failure test that selects a PENDING item, simulates another Worker committing COMPLETED before the research DB call fails, then asserts the item and parent deletion job remain COMPLETED.
- **Files:** worker/account-lifecycle/workerLifecycleRuntime.ts; tests/rt-db-triad-001.test.ts.
- **Verification:** Source and USER_DB migration contract reviewed. Test/typecheck not run because MacBook remains Offline. Status: IMPLEMENTED_BUT_NOT_VERIFIED.
- **Code head:** 043e25c7c044cf4375fd40b68bbd62bb02c8831a. Execution-state commit: 15764b32b5268253ed59a9df52b902b3ee398190.
- **CI:** Latest four workflows on this head failed (runs 45986606294, 45986606317, 45986606333, 45986606330); logs and step metadata remain unavailable. Root cause unknown.

## 2026-11-27 — Outbox unavailable-DB race fence
- **Finding:** processPendingResearchErasureOutbox had a second stale-worker downgrade path when RESEARCH_DB was absent. It updated status to RETRY_PENDING using only outbox_id, so an invocation that had scanned PENDING could overwrite a competing worker's COMPLETED state.
- **Fix:** The unavailable-DB retry update is now conditional on status IN (PENDING, RETRY_PENDING); attempt_count increments atomically in SQL. If the guarded update changes no row, the code reads the canonical outbox state and treats a valid COMPLETED row as completed rather than pending.
- **Regression:** Added a SQLite-backed race test that changes the selected row to COMPLETED between scan and unavailable-DB update, then asserts the completion receipt and parent deletion job remain COMPLETED.
- **Files:** worker/account-lifecycle/workerLifecycleRuntime.ts; tests/rt-db-triad-001.test.ts.

## 2026-11-27 — Concurrent outbox retry counter hardening and consent-barrier audit

- **Finding 1:** The outbox failure path computed `attempts` from the row scan and wrote that stale value back. Two overlapping failed workers could both scan attempt_count=0 and both write 1, losing one retry increment. The unavailable-RESEARCH_DB path was already using an atomic increment, but its backoff timestamp could still be overwritten by an earlier retry time.
- **Fix:** Both retry paths now increment `attempt_count` in SQL, calculate the exponential backoff from the current row value inside the UPDATE, and use `MAX(next_retry_at_ms, ...)` so a later retry deadline cannot be shortened. The success transition also increments atomically and only updates PENDING/RETRY_PENDING rows.
- **Regression:** Added a deterministic overlapping-worker test in `tests/rt-db-triad-001.test.ts`. It forces two invocations to read the same PENDING row before either retry update, injects RESEARCH_DB failure, then asserts attempt_count=2 and the 40-second backoff corresponding to the second attempt.
- **Files:** `worker/account-lifecycle/workerLifecycleRuntime.ts`; `tests/rt-db-triad-001.test.ts`.
- **Commits:** source `e95035c297ffee5a667b79328efd25096d4a9809`; regression `d0faff86e79dde909faeb3cacbfa57e65e55ae37`.
- **Verification:** Re-read both updated files and checked the final SQL bindings and test interleaving. Tests and typecheck were NOT run: local executor is Offline. GitHub runs 45990057254, 45990057424, 45990057265 and 45990057325 all failed with runner_id=0 and zero steps; no code-level checks executed.
- **Finding 2:** RT-DB-TRIAD-003 consent revocation primitives/migrations are not integrated into active runtime. HTTP ingestion lacks leased permits; Queue ingestion uses the older consent helper; event writes omit consent_revision and fence checks; privacy route updates consent directly without starting/draining/reconciling revocation; dormant tests expect APIs not exported by the active service.
- **Decision:** Do not claim consent revocation erasure is complete. Next implementation package is the full permit + revision fence + revocation outbox orchestration across HTTP, Queue and account privacy routes, followed by activating and running the six-scenario TRIAD-003 suite.
- **CI diagnosis:** Updated issue #49 with fresh PR #67/#79 runner evidence earlier in this session. PR #78 adds another reproduction on the exact code/test head above. The runner problem remains account-level and unresolved; no blind reruns or workflow permission weakening.

## 2026-11-27 — RT-DB-TRIAD-003 consent barrier implementation pass
- **Implemented:** Direct HTTP and Queue research ingestion now acquire bounded USER_DB permits tied to consent_revision and release them in finally. Research event inserts carry consent_revision and enforce RESEARCH_DB subject fences in the INSERT statement.
- **Implemented:** Added durable revocation orchestration. It persists revocation job + UID-free revision-scoped outbox before flipping consent off, so a crash in between fails closed and can be recovered by reconcile. Active permits drain; expired leases are reclaimed. Re-grant is blocked until the revocation outbox has verifiable receipts.
- **Implemented:** Revision-scoped erasure creates Inbox receipt count, invalidates aggregation windows, deletes events and advances the subject fence in one RESEARCH_DB batch. Full account deletion still uses NULL max_consent_revision and remains distinct.
- **Routes/contracts:** PATCH /api/account/privacy returns 202 while permits drain; POST /api/account/privacy/reconcile resumes durable jobs. Added to Worker route group and API catalog.
- **Tests:** Activated tests/rt-db-triad-003-consent-barrier.test.ts in npm run test:triad; added explicit pending-regrant rejection assertion. ## 2026-11-27 — TRIAD-003 CAS, uniqueness and legacy race-test alignment
- **Concurrency hardening:** Added USER_DB migration 0008 with a unique key on (uid, revoked_consent_revision) and a retry-scan index. Revocation IDs and outbox IDs are deterministic per UID/revision/subject; concurrent duplicate revocations converge on the same job/outbox.
- **Consent CAS:** updateUserPrivacyConsentInD1 now supports expectedConsentRevision. Revocation intent is committed before the consent CAS; if another privacy mutation wins first, the unapplied intent/outbox is canceled. While a revocation is pending, unrelated consent mutations and re-grants are rejected.
- **Test alignment:** Updated the existing TRIAD-002 mid-flight race test to the leased-permit contract: an already-permitted request may finish, then reconciliation erases its event and advances the fence. It no longer expects a pre-commit 403 that conflicts with permit-drain semantics. TRIAD-003 fixture now applies eight USER_DB migrations.
- **Default test entrypoint:** npm test now invokes npm run test:triad; test:triad includes TRIAD-001, TRIAD-002 and TRIAD-003.
- **Verification:** Source, migration and test changes re-read from GitHub. No tests/typecheck executed. Latest Quality Gates run 45995787426 has runner_id=0, empty runner_name and zero steps; Backend Triad run 45995787483 and Native Auth run 45995787609 have null step metadata. E2E skipped. This is still the runner-allocation blocker in issue #49, not evidence of source test failure.
- **Status:** IMPLEMENTED_BUT_NOT_VERIFIED. PR #78 remains Draft; no merge/deploy/production migration.


## 2026-11-27 — TRIAD-003 post-snapshot race closure
- **Race closure:** Revocation no longer marks a job complete from the initial registry snapshot. The flow always enters reconciliation; only after all permits drain does it re-read the registry and create missing UID-free, revision-scoped outbox rows. Empty-outbox completion occurs only after this second scan.
- **Concurrency:** Deterministic revocation/outbox IDs converge concurrent duplicate requests; expectedConsentRevision CAS and stale-intent cleanup prevent a losing privacy update from leaving an orphaned active job.
- **Regression:** Added Scenario 7, which injects a completed research ingestion and active permit between the initial registry read and revocation commit; after permit release, reconciliation must discover the late registry row, erase the event and complete the job. SQL template literal syntax was re-read and corrected.
- **CI routing:** Radar Backend Triad path filters now include tests/rt-db-triad-001.test.ts and tests/rt-db-triad-003-consent-barrier.test.ts. npm test now invokes all three DB triad suites through test:triad.
